公司簡介
CORPORATE PROFILE

四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「四環醫藥」                Founded in 2001, Sihuan Pharmaceutical Holdings Group Ltd. (“Sihuan
或「本公司」，連同其附屬公司為「本集團」）              Pharmaceutical” or the “Company”), together with its subsidiaries (the
成立於二零零一年，自二零零七年起，成                 “Group”) has become the largest cardio-cerebral vascular (“CCV”) drug
為全國最大的心腦血管（「心腦血管」）處方               manufacturer in China’s prescription drug market by market share since 2007.
藥物市場的藥物生產商。四環醫藥為自家                 Sihuan Pharmaceutical is an integrated pharmaceutical corporation with its
生產、研究與開發（「研發」）、營銷一體的               own production, research and development (“R&D”) and marketing
醫藥公司。本集團的成功是由於擁有優秀                 capacities. The success of the Group can be attributed to its differentiated
的行銷模式及覆蓋全國的分銷網路、具市                 and proven sales and marketing model, extensive nationwide distribution
場潛力的多元化產品組合，以及強大的研                 network, diversified product portfolio, and strong R&D capabilities.
發實力。

本集團於二零零八年開始投入創新藥研                  The Group has begun its innovative drug R&D since 2008 and now possesses
發，擁有約 600 名研發人員組成的領先的              a well-established innovative and generic drug R&D platform, which is
研發團隊，已建立了成熟的創新藥和仿製                 supported by approximately 600 R&D experts and focuses in therapeutic
藥研發平台，專注於具有重大臨床需求的                 areas with considerable unmet clinical demand. The Group’s current products
治療領域。本集團目前銷售產品及在研發                 and R&D pipeline encompass the top five medical therapeutic areas: CCV
產 品 線 涵 蓋 五 大 醫 療 領 域： 心 腦 血 管 系   system, central nervous system, metabolism, oncology and anti-infectives.
統、中樞神經系統、新陳代謝、腫瘤及抗
感染。

                                                           CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT


二零一九年，是中國醫藥行業大變革的時                 2019 was an era of tremendous changes in China’s pharmaceutical industry.
代。一系列有關醫藥產業深入變革的政策                 A series of major policies related to the in-depth reform of the pharmaceutical
如國家藥品集中採購、國家重點監控合理                 industry, such as the National Centralized Drug Procurement, National
用藥藥品目錄（化藥及生物製品）（「重點監               Catalog of Drugs under Close Monitoring of Rational Drug Use (for Chemical
控藥品目錄」）、國家醫保目錄調整、藥品                Medicines and Biological Products) (the “Key Monitoring Drug List”)
談判目錄及新的藥品管理法等多項重大政                 adjustment to the National Reimbursement Drug List, the Drug Negotiation
策相繼出台，進一步深化醫療制度，並且                 List as well as the new Drug Administration Law, have been introduced to
推進中國醫藥行業進入新的發展階段。其                 further deepen the healthcare system and promote China’s pharmaceutical
中由中國國家醫療保障局主導的新一輪帶                 industry to usher in a new stage of development. Among them, a new round
量採購，以前所未有的力度大幅壓縮包括                 of centralized procurement led by the National Healthcare Security
創新藥在內的藥品利潤空間，從而加速醫                 Administration in the PRC has greatly reduced the profit margin of drugs,
藥行業的重構。                            including innovative drugs, with unprecedented strength, thereby
                                   accelerating the restructuring of the pharmaceutical industry.

面對整個醫藥行業環境的巨大改變，作為                 Facing the drastic changes in the environment of the entire pharmaceutical
行業中的一員，四環醫藥難以獨善其身，                 industry, Sihuan Pharmaceutical, as a member of the industry, could hardly
但從另一個角度看，如能在危機和競爭壓                 remain immune. However, from another perspective, if Sihuan
力下，大膽變革，讓組織和隊伍在新的發                 Pharmaceutical is able to boldly carry out transformation under such crisis
展階段下充滿活力和鬥志，經過火的洗禮                 and competitive pressure to bring vitality to, and enhance morale of, its
而涅槃重生，從此步入「二次創業」的新征                organization and team under the new stage of development, it would be the
途，也未嘗不是恰逢其時的好時機。                   right time to restart a new journey of “second entrepreneurship”.

故 今 年 的 報 告 凝 結 起 來 就 是 三 個 詞： 聚   Therefore, this year’s report could be summarized as follows: Focus, change
焦、變革和社會責任。                         and social responsibility.

       「致力於成為最具競爭力的國際                          “Dedicated in becoming the most competitive international

A. 業務回顧                             A. BUSINESS REVIEW
  二零一九年，是中國醫藥行業大變革                     2019 was an era of tremendous changes in China’s pharmaceutical
  的時代。一系列有關醫藥產業深入變                     industry. A series of major policies related to the in-depth reform of the
  革的政策如國家藥品集中採購、重點                     pharmaceutical industry, such as the National Centralized Drug
  監控藥品目錄、國家醫保目錄調整、                     Procurement, Key Monitoring Drug List, adjustment to the National
  藥品談判目錄及新的藥品管理法等多                     Reimbursement Drug List, the Drug Negotiation List as well as the new
  項重大政策相繼出台，進一步深化醫                     Drug Administration Law, have been introduced to further deepen the
  療制度，並且推進中國醫藥行業進入                     healthcare system and promote China’s pharmaceutical industry to
  新的發展階段。其中由中國國家醫療                     usher in a new stage of development. Among them, a new round of
  保障局主導的新一輪帶量採購，以前                     centralized procurement led by the National Healthcare Security
  所未有的力度大幅壓縮包括創新藥在                     Administration in the PRC has greatly reduced the profit margin of
  內的藥品利潤空間，從而加速醫藥行                     drugs, including innovative drugs, with unprecedented strength,
  業的重構。                                thereby accelerating the restructuring of the pharmaceutical industry.

     其中，於二零一九年七月頒佈的重點                   consolidation and revolution of the pharmaceutical industry. Further

     模式帶來進一步的影響。                        envisioned after the promulgation of the Key Monitoring Drug List in

良好的企業管治有利於提高本公司的整體                  Good corporate governance is favorable for the improvement of the overall
表現、透明度及問責性，對於現代化企業                  performance, transparency and accountability of the Company and is crucial
管 治 尤 為 重 要。 董 事 會 以 股 東 利 益 為 依    to the modern corporate administration. The Board, in the interests of the
歸，致力維持及確保高水平之企業管治。                  Shareholders, devotes considerable efforts to maintaining and ensuring a
                                    high standard of corporate governance.

企業管治守則                              CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CODE
本公司認識到公司的透明度及問責之重要                  The Company recognises the importance of corporate transparency and
性。本公司致力於偏現高標準的企業管治                  accountability. The Company is committed in achieving a high standard of
及憑藉行之有效的企業管治流程，帶領本                  corporate governance and leading the Group to attain better results and
集團取得良好業績及提高企業形象。                    improve its corporate image with effective corporate governance procedures.

於報告期內本公司已遵守上市規則附錄                   The Company has complied with all the applicable code provisions as set out

適用守則條文。                             the Listing Rules during the reporting period.

 董事會由五名執行董事、一名非執行董事                 The Board comprises five executive Directors, one non-executive Director and
 及三名獨立非執行董事組成。除本年報披                 three independent non-executive Directors. Save as disclosed in this annual
 露外，董事會成員之間並無任何財務、業                 report, there is no financial, business, family or other material relationship
 務、家族或其他重要關係。董事會成員之                 among the members of the Board. The biographical information of the
 履 歷 載 於 第 51 至 58 頁 之「董 事 履 歷」。年   members of the Board are set out on pages 51 to 58 in the section headed
 內，主席與行政總裁的職責已區分，且該                 “Biographical Details of Directors”. During the Year, the roles of the Chairman

（作為行政總裁）行使。                         by Dr. Che Fengsheng as the Chairman and Dr. Guo Weicheng as the Chief
                                    Executive Officer.

董事會負責監管本集團的戰略發展，決定                  The Board is responsible for the governance of the Group’s strategic
本集團的目標、商務策略及政策，並監察                  development, determination of the Group’s objectives, business strategies
及掌控營運及財務表現，以達到本集團的                  and policies, and the supervision and control of the operation and financial
戰略性目標。本集團的管理團隊具備廣泛                  performance in pursuit of the Group’s strategic objectives. Our management
的營運經驗及行業知識，並已獲董事會授                  team, which possesses extensive operating experience and industry

報管理層和董事會。本集團進一步制定風                                         of staff, allocation of resources and performance appraisal, and a written

股東溝通政策                             Shareholder Communication Policy
根據守則要求，董事會已採納股東溝通政                 The Board adopted a shareholder communication policy according to the
策。                                 Code.

1.   目的                            1.   Purpose
     1.1 本政策所載條文旨在確保四環                  1.1 This Policy aims to set out the provisions with the objective of
         集團股東，包括個人及機構股                       ensuring that Sihuan’s shareholders, both individual and
         東（統稱「股東」），及在適當情                     institutional (collectively, “Shareholders”), and, in appropriate
         況下包括一般投資人士，均可                       circumstances, the investment community at large, are provided
         適時取得全面、相同及容易理                       with ready, equal and timely access to balanced and
         解的本公司資料（包括其財務表                      understandable information about the Company (including its
         現、戰略目標及計劃、重大發                       financial performance, strategic goals and plans, material
         展、管治及風險概況），一方面                      developments, governance and risk profile), in order to enable
         使股東可在知情情況下行使權                       Shareholders to exercise their rights in an informed manner, and
         力，另一方面也讓股東及投資                       to allow Shareholders and the investment community to engage
         人士與本公司加強溝通。                         actively with the Company.

     1.2   就本政策而言，包括本公司的                1.2   For the purpose of this Policy, references to the investment
           準投資者，以及就本公司表現                      community is intended to include the Company’s potential
           進行報告及分析的分析員。                       investors as well as analysts reporting and analysing the
                                              Company’s performance.

                                                        REPORT OF DIRECTORS


董事會欣然提呈本年度董事會報告及經審        The Board is pleased to present the Directors’ report together with the

                          Statements”).

主要業務                      PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES
本公司為一家投資控股公司，本公司附屬        The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activities of
公司之主要業務載列於財務報表附註 12。      its subsidiaries are set out in Note 12 to the Financial Statements.

業務審視                      BUSINESS REVIEW
主要關係                      Key Relationships
i. 僱員                     (i)  Employees
   僱員為本集團最大資產之一，本集團            Employees are one of the greatest assets of the Group and the Group
   高度重視僱員之個人發展。本集團定            regards the personal development of its employees highly important.
   期檢討僱員的薪酬待遇並作出必要調            The Group regularly reviews the remuneration package of employees
   整以符合市場標準，亦已採納購股期            and makes necessary adjustments to conform to the market standard.
   權計劃及股份獎勵計劃，以表揚及獎            The Group has also adopted share option scheme and share award
   勵員工對本集團之營運及未來發展作            scheme to recognize and reward the contribution of the employees for
   出的貢獻。                       the benefit of the Group’s operations and future development.

環境保護                                                       ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION
本公司深明環境保護是實現企業可持續發                                         The Company recognises the importance of environmental protection as part
展的重要組成。在生產與運營過程中，本                                         of corporate sustainability. In the process of production and operation, the
集團嚴格遵守業務所在地的環保法律法                                          Group strictly abides by environmental protection laws and regulations of
規， 持 續 健 全 並 執 行 環 境 管 理 的 制 度 體                           places where it operates and continues to improve and implement
系，加強環境管理的監督機制，確保全面                                         environmental management systems, strengthens environmental
合規排放。本年度，本集團重點推進下屬                                         management monitoring mechanism and ensures full compliance regarding
生產單位的環境管理標準化體系建設，加                                         emissions. During the Year, the Group focused on carrying forward the
強環境風險防範；同時，積極開展如鍋爐                                         establishment of a standardized environmental management system of its
煤改氣等環保改造項目，務求減少企業經                                         subordinate production units to strengthen the prevention of environmental
營活動的環境影響。                                                  risks. At the same time, it actively carried out environmental protection
                                                           transformation projects, such as switching over from coal to gas for boilers, to
                                                           reduce the environmental impact of its operations.

本集團宣導綠色環保理念，積極推行綠色                                         The Group promotes the concept of environmental protection, actively
辦公各項措施，優選節能電子設備，加強                                         implements green office measures, optimizes energy-saving electronic
能 耗 管 理， 鼓 勵 員 工 培 養 節 能 與 節 水 習                           equipment, strengthens energy consumption management and encourages
慣，致力實現節能減排的環保目標。                                           employees to cultivate energy-saving and water-saving habits, with an aim to
                                                           achieving environmental protection goals of energy conservation and

本集團將不時檢討其環保工作，並在業務                                         The Group will review its environmental protection work from time to time

授購股權的合資格人士、每份購股權所涉        Eligible Persons to whom Share Options shall be granted, the number of

制或局限。釐定認購價的基準亦於購股權        limitations imposed pursuant to the rules of the Share Option Scheme. The

現目標。董事認為，讓董事會擁有酌情權        rules of the Share Option Scheme. There is no performance target specified in

目標及認購價，將可更有效地達成購股權        Option must be held before it can be exercised, performance targets and the
計劃的目的，原因是這可讓董事會因應承        subscription price, upon the grant of Share Options will better serve the

經驗、過往工作表現、專業領域等因素後        Share Options that cater to the specific circumstances of the Grantee, taking

勵及激勵。                     field of expertise, etc., and thereby providing appropriate motivation and

惟本彌償保證不延伸至任何上述人士與欺                     dishonesty which may attach to any of the said persons.
詐或不忠誠有關的事宜。

本公司已安排合適的責任保險，以就董事                     The Company has arranged appropriate liability insurance to indemnify the
因公司業務而產生的責任向彼等提供彌償。                    Directors for their liabilities arising out of corporate activities.

於二零一六年七月十五日，安永獲首次委                 Ernst & Young was first appointed as new auditor of the Company on 15 July
任為本公司之新核數師填補因國富浩華辭                 2016 to fill the casual vacancy following the resignation of Crowe Horwath.
任產生的出缺。於二零一九年五月三十一                 Ernst & Young was appointed again as the auditor at the annual general
日本公司之股東週年大會，安永獲再次委                 meeting of the Company held on 31 May 2019.
任為本公司之新核數師。

本公司截至二零一八年十二月三十一日止                 The consolidated financial statements of the Company for the year ended 31
年度的綜合財務報表乃由安永審核。                   December 2018 were audited by Ernst & Young.

安永將退任，並合資格膺選連任。本公司                 Ernst & Young will retire and being eligible, offer themselves for re-election. A
將於應屆股東週年大會上提呈一項決議案                 resolution for the reappointment of Ernst & Young as auditor of the Company
續聘安永為本公司之核數師。                      will be proposed at the forthcoming annual general meeting.

我們的目標是就綜合財務報表整體是否不                                       Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the
存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳                                       consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material

任。

合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按                                       Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that
照國際審計準則進行的審核在某重大錯誤                                       an audit conducted in accordance with ISAs will always detect a material
陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可能由欺                                       misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and
詐或錯誤引起，而倘合理預期彼等單獨或                                       are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could
總體可能影響使用者依賴該等綜合財務報                                       reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken
表所作出的經濟決定，則有關錯誤陳述被                                       on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.
視為重大。

作為我們根據國際審計準則進行審核的一                                       As part of an audit in accordance with ISAs, we exercise professional
部分，在審核過程中我們運用專業判斷及                                       judgement and maintain professional scepticism throughout the audit. We
保持專業懷疑態度。我們亦：                                            also:

•   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜                                     •       Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the
    合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風                                              consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error,
    險，設計及執行審核程序以應對該等                                             design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks, and
    風險，以及獲取充足及適當的審核憑                                             obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate to provide a
    證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐                                             basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material misstatement
    可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛                                             resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud
    假陳述，或淩駕於內部控制之上，因                                             may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions,
    此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤                                             misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.
    陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導
    致的重大錯誤陳述的風險。

•   了解與審核相關的內部控制，以設計                                     •       Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit in
    適當的審核程序，但目的並非對 貴                                             order to design audit procedures that are appropriate in the
    集團內部控制的有效性發表意見。                                              circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an opinion on the

             租賃的新定義                                 New definition of a lease
             根據國際財務報告準則第                            Under IFRS 16, a contract is, or contains, a lease if the
             16 號，倘合約為換取代價                          contract conveys a right to control the use of an identified
             而給予在一段時間內控制                            asset for a period of time in exchange for consideration.
             已識別資產使用的權利，                            Control is conveyed where the customer has both the
             則該合約為租賃或包含租                            right to obtain substantially all of the economic benefits
             賃。倘客戶同時有權獲取                            from use of the identified asset and the right to direct the
             使用已識別資產帶來的絕                            use of the identified asset. The Group elected to use the
             大部分經濟利益及有權指                            transition practical expedient allowing the standard to be
             示已識別資產的用途，則                            applied only to contracts that were previously identified as
             擁有控制權。本集團選擇                            leases applying IAS 17 and IFRIC 4 at the date of initial
             使用過渡實用權宜之計，                            application. Contracts that were not identified as leases
             讓該準則僅被應用於先前                            under IAS 17 and IFRIC 4 were not reassessed. Therefore,
             於首次應用日期採用國際                            the definition of a lease under IFRS 16 has been applied
             會 計 準 則 第 17 號 及 國 際                   only to contracts entered into or changed on or after 1
             財務報告詮釋委員會第 4                           January 2019.
             號被確認為租賃之合約。
             根據國際會計準則第 17 號
             及國際財務報告詮釋委員
             會第 4 號並未確認為租賃
             之合約將不進行重估。因
             此，根據國際財務報告準
             則 第 16 號 的 租 賃 定 義 僅
             適用於二零一九年一月一
             日或之後訂立或更改的合
             約。

                 將該等長期權益入賬時，                                                       applied to the net investment, which includes the long-
                 實體應用國際財務報告準                                                       term interests, only in the context of recognising losses of

                 於聯營公司或合營企業的                                                       long-term interests in associates and joint ventures

           報告準則  （續）
           國際財務報告準則第3號的修                       Amendments to IFRS 3 clarify and provide additional guidance
           訂釐清業務的定義及提供有關                       on the definition of a business. The amendments clarify that for
           定 義 的 額 外 指 引。 該 修 訂 釐              an integrated set of activities and assets to be considered a
           清，一組綜合活動及資產須至                       business, it must include, at a minimum, an input and a
           少包括一項參數與一個重要過                       substantive process that together significantly contribute to the
           程，且兩者須共同對創造收益                       ability to create output. A business can exist without including
           的能力作出重大貢獻，方能被                       all of the inputs and processes needed to create outputs. The
           視為一項業務。在並未包含所                       amendments remove the assessment of whether market
           有創造收益的參數及過程的情                       participants are capable of acquiring the business and continue
           況下，業務亦可存續。該修訂                       to produce outputs. Instead, the focus is on whether acquired
           取消對市場參與者是否具備能                       inputs and acquired substantive processes together significantly
           力收購業務及持續產生收益進                       contribute to the ability to create outputs. The amendments
           行的評估，相反，其重點放在                       have also narrowed the definition of outputs to focus on goods
           已取得的參數及已取得的重要                       or services provided to customers, investment income or other
           過程能否共同對創造收益的能                       income from ordinary activities. Furthermore, the amendments
           力作出重大貢獻。該修訂亦收                       provide guidance to assess whether an acquired process is
           窄收益的定義，聚焦向客戶提                       substantive and introduce an optional fair value concentration
           供的貨品或服務、投資收入或                       test to permit a simplified assessment of whether an acquired
           來自日常活動的其他收入。此                       set of activities and assets is not a business. The Group expects
           外，該修訂提供有關評估已取                       to adopt the amendments prospectively from 1 January 2020.
           得的過程是否屬重大的指引，                       Since the amendments apply prospectively to transactions or
           並引入選擇性公平值集中度測                       other events that occur on or after the date of first application,
           試，允許對一組已收購活動及                       the Group will not be affected by these amendments on the
           資產是否屬一項業務進行簡單                       date of transition.
           評估。本集團預期將於二零二
           零年一月一日起提前採納該修
           訂。由於該等修訂預期適用於
           首次應用日期或之後發生之交

             值計量。收購相關成本於產生

           公平值，則於重新評估後其差
           額將於損益內確認為議價收購
           收益。

           於初步確認後，商譽按成本值                       After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less any
           減任何累計減值虧損計量。商                       accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for
           譽須每年進行減值測試，倘出                       impairment annually or more frequently if events or changes in
           現任何事件或情況有變顯示賬                       circumstances indicate that the carrying value may be impaired.
           面值可能減少，則會更頻密地                       The Group performs its annual impairment test of goodwill as at
           進 行 測 試。 本 集 團 於 十 二 月              31 December. For the purpose of impairment testing, goodwill
           三十一日進行商譽之年度減值                       acquired in a business combination is, from the acquisition date,
           測試。為進行減值測試，因業                       allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating units, or groups
           務合併而收購之商譽乃自收購                       of cash-generating units, that are expected to benefit from the
           日期起分配至預期可自合併之                       synergies of the combination, irrespective of whether other

2.    主要會計政策概要（續）                                              2.      SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
                                                                       POLICIES (Continued)
      2.6    公平值計量                                                     2.6     Fair value measurement
             本集團於各報告期末按公平值                                                     The Group measures its equity investments at fair value at the
             計量其股本投資。公平值為市                                                     end of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that would
             場參與者於計量日在有序交易                                                     be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an
             中出售資產所收取之價格或轉                                                     orderly transaction between market participants at the
             讓負債所支付之價格。公平值                                                     measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the
             計量乃根據假設出售資產或轉                                                     presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the
             讓負債之交易在資產或負債之                                                     liability takes place either in the principal market for the asset or
             主要市場或（在無主要市場之情                                                    liability, or in the absence of a principal market, in the most
             況下）在資產或負債之最具優勢                                                    advantageous market for the asset or liability. The principal or
             市場進行而作出。主要或最具                                                     the most advantageous market must be accessible by the Group.
             優勢市場必須為本集團可進入                                                     The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using the
             之市場。資產或負債之公平值                                                     assumptions that market participants would use when pricing
             乃按假設市場參與者於資產或                                                     the asset or liability, assuming that market participants act in
             負債定價時會以最佳經濟利益                                                     their economic best interest.
             行事計量。

             非金融資產之公平值計量經計                                                     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into
             及市場參與者透過以最大限度                                                     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic
             利用資產以達致最佳用途或將                                                     benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by
             資產出售予將以最大限度利用                                                     selling it to another market participant that would use the asset
             資產以達致最佳用途之另一名                                                     in its highest and best use.
             市場參與者而產生經濟效益之
             能力。

             本集團使用在屬適當情況下且                                                     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the
             具備充足數據可供計量公平值                                                     circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to
             之估值方法，以盡量增加使用                                                     measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable
             相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減                                                     inputs and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.
             少使用不可觀察輸入數據。

             所有其公平值會被計量或於財                                                     All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or
             務報表披露之資產及負債乃按                                                     disclosed in the financial statements are categorised within the
             整體對公平值計量屬重要之最                                                     fair value hierarchy, described as follows, based on the lowest
             低級別輸入數據在下述公平值                                                     level input that is significant to the fair value measurement as a
             等級內分類：                                                            whole:

             第一級 — 基於相同資產或負                                                    Level 1 — based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active
                   債於活躍市場之報                                                              markets for identical assets or liabilities
                   價（未經調整）
             第二級 — 基於對公平值計量                                                    Level 2 — based on valuation techniques for which the lowest
                   屬重要之最低級別                                                              level input that is significant to the fair value
                   輸入數據均可直接                                                              measurement is observable, either directly or
                   或間接被觀察之估                                                              indirectly
                   值方法
             第三級 — 基於對公平值計量                                                    Level 3 — based on valuation techniques for which the lowest
                   屬重要之最低級別                                                              level input that is significant to the fair value
                   輸入數據為不可觀                                                              measurement is unobservable
                   察之估值方法

              額於綜合收益表確認。                      consolidated statement of profit or loss.

           該類別包括本集團並無不                        This category includes derivative instruments and equity

           入其他全面收益進行分類                        to classify at fair value through other comprehensive
           的衍生工具及股本投資。                        income. Dividends on equity investments classified as

      2.25 合約負債                                                       2.25 Contract liabilities
           合約負債於本集團轉移相關貨                                                   A contract liability is recognised when a payment is received or a
           品或服務之前收到客戶付款或                                                   payment is due (whichever is earlier) from a customer before the
           有關款項到期（以較早者為準）                                                  Group transfers the related goods or services. Contract liabilities

           益。

      2.26 合約成本                                                       2.26 Contract costs
           除資本化為存貨、物業，廠房                                                   Other than the costs which are capitalised as inventories,
           及設備及無形資產之成本外，                                                   property, plant and equipment and intangible assets, costs
           為履行與客戶的合約所產生的                                                   incurred to fulfill a contract with a customer are capitalised as an

           資本化為資產：

            (a)     該成本與實體可具體識別                                               (a)     The costs relate directly to a contract or to an anticipated
                    的一項當前或預期取得的                                                       contract that the entity can specifically identify.
                    合約直接相關。

                    未來用於履行（或持續履                                                       will be used in satisfying (or in continuing to satisfy)
                    行）履約義務的資源。                                                        performance obligations in the future.

            (c)     該成本預期能夠收回。                                                (c)     The costs are expected to be recovered.

            資本化合約成本乃按符合確認                                                     The capitalised contract costs are amortised and charged to the
            相關資產的收益模式的系統基                                                     statement of profit or loss on a systematic basis that is consistent
            準攤銷並自損益表扣除。其他                                                     with the pattern of the revenue to which the asset related is
            合約成本於產生時支銷。                                                       recognised. Other contract costs are expensed as incurred.

              適用匯率換算為功能貨                           the dates of the transactions or valuation where items are
              幣。該等交易結算以及以                          re-measured. Foreign exchange gains and losses resulting

              「其他開支」或「其他收益                         comprehensive income within “other expenses” or “other

              以外幣計值分類為可供出                          Changes in the fair value of debt securities denominated

              動及證券賬面值之其他變                          changes in the amortised cost of the securities and other

              確認。                                  income.

           估 值 要 求 董 事 根 據 行 業、 規              requires the directors to determine comparable public

           例 如 企 業 價 值 對 除 利 息、 稅              (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”) multiple, for

           數。倍數乃以可資比公司的企                       an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then discounted for
           業價值除以盈利衡量計算得                        considerations such as illiquidity and size differences between
           出。交易倍數以各可資比公司                       the comparable companies based on company-specific facts
           根據公司獨有因素及情況的考                       and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the
           慮因素，例如非流通性及規模                       corresponding earnings measure of the unlisted equity
           差異，予以貼現。貼現倍數用                       investments to measure the fair value. The directors believe that
           於非上市股權投資的相應盈利                       the estimated fair values resulting from the valuation technique,

11. 長期資產（包括商譽）減值測                                                   11. IMPAIRMENT TESTING OF LONG-TERM ASSETS
    試                                                                   INCLUDING GOODWILL
      通過業務合併收購的商譽會就減值測                                                       Goodwill acquired through business combinations is allocated to the
      試分配至所收購附屬公司現金產生單                                                       acquired subsidiaries, which are CGUs, for impairment testing. The
      位。現金產生單位的可收回款額按使                                                       recoverable amount of the CGU has been determined based on a
      用價值計算法釐定，該計算法乃基於                                                       value-in-use calculation using cash flow projections based on financial
      管理層所批准涵蓋五年期的財政預算                                                       budgets covering a five-year period approved by management. Cash
      案採用現金流量預測計算。五年期以                                                       flows beyond the five-year period are extrapolated using the estimated
      後的現金流量則採用下述估計增長率                                                       growth rates stated below. The growth rate does not exceed the long-
      進行推斷。增長率並無超出現金產生                                                       term average growth rate for the pharmaceutical business in which the
      單位營運所屬醫藥業務的長期平均增                                                       CGU operates.
      長率。

     分配至各現金產生單位的長期資產                                                         The carrying amounts of long-term assets including goodwill allocated
    （包括商譽）賬面值如下：                                                             to each of the CGUs are as follows:

  成立的外資企業向海外投資者所宣派                                     is levied on dividends declared to foreign investors from the foreign
  股息須繳納 10% 預扣稅。此規定自二                                  investment enterprises established in Mainland China. The requirement
  零零八年一月一日起生效，並適用於                                     is effective from 1 January 2008 and applies to earnings after 31
  二零零七年十二月三十一日後之盈                                      December 2007. A lower withholding tax rate may be applied if there is
  利。較低的預扣稅率或會於中國內地                                     a tax treaty between Mainland China and the jurisdiction of the foreign
  與海外投資者司法權區訂有稅務條約                                     investors. For the Group, the applicable rate is 5%. The Group is
  之情況下適用。就本集團而言，適用                                     therefore liable for withholding taxes on dividends distributed by those
  稅率為 5%。本集團因此須就其在中                                    subsidiaries established in Mainland China in respect of earnings
  國內地成立的附屬公司於二零零八年                                     generated from 1 January 2008.

  雙重稅務安排的條件或規定，則有關                       entered into between the PRC and Hong Kong, the relevant
  預扣稅率由 10% 減至 5%。                       withholding tax rate will be reduced to 5% from 10%.
